# Prefix classification — model eval

274 gold forms (manual overrides). Binary: prefix vs. non-prefix (root+formative).

- **rules**: 226/274 correct (82%)
- **joint**: 221/268 correct (82%) · 6 skipped
- **dictalm**: 174/274 correct (64%)
- **dictalm ensemble eligibility**: excluded; produced only prefix

Ensemble rules+joint over 268 rows where both scored:
- **union** (either says prefix): 239/268 (89%)
- **intersection** (agree-on-prefix, else non-prefix): 206/268 (77%)
- **disagree** on 75 rows → rules right 39, joint right 36, neither 0
